## Records-only promotion PRs (19 October 2026)

[Plan section 24](plan.md#records-only-prs-decision-b-operator-19-october-2026-stages-started-19-october-2026), B2. **Done, including the live promotion and rollback proofs:** initial implementation `e3a76edf` (console image `src-95249ba6c9ec3ef1`, pin `08a90bb6`), corrected by `47bc584e` (console image `src-df89d769218e5391`, pin `06dbe76e`). [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39316579126) passed; `make flux-reconcile` and `make verify-platform` passed, and both checker jobs succeeded on that image. The preceding local `make check` passed (825 tests); one earlier run failed in the unchanged SQLite backup test, which passed three isolated reruns and the next complete run. A scratch-clone rehearsal using real Git (no pushes) merged a records-only `demo-orders` promotion onto an unrelated newer commit, found the digest in staging's history, rendered its HelmRelease and passed `check-apps` for all 16 instances.

**Throwaway:** the operator approved public repository `swhurl-try-30` for these proofs. `make app-repo TYPE=worker` created it and installed its image webhook. `23bd6cd4` added staging (team `insights`, private, no database or app Secret). `make check` passed before that commit. Reconcile passed; Deploy's `ba6047b3` replaced the seed with build `1-3cbe7bb`, digest `b9dc9318…`; `make app-status` showed Healthy and its worker logged `WORK DONE`.

**Activity regression found during the proof:** a held later promotion merged with GitHub's button leaves the old HelmRelease beside the new records until Render runs. Activity must not call the healthy previous image Live or persist a first-seen time. The regression changes only production's Release record in a mock GitHub tree, leaving the old HelmRelease, and asserts `applying` with `main has not rendered it yet`; it failed before the fix. A rendered digest different from the requested record's digest now means `unrendered`, as does a missing HelmRelease. A pending promotion whose records have already been replaced is superseded instead of waiting indefinitely.

**Gate review corrections before the live proof:** matching a digest anywhere in Git history was insufficient: a PR could retag a historical rollback as a newer build or change unchecked Release fields while keeping a known digest. The gate now compares the entire promoted Release against parsed staging records on `main`'s first-parent history (including refreshed records that kept the digest); the console uses the same rule against its current staging Release. Regressions cover a retagged rollback, altered health path/UID and a digest present only in a comment. Failed history reads, fatal merges without unmerged paths, and failed render/check commands remain operational failures (label intact), not permanent manual holds; only confirmed conflicts and the domain rule hold a PR. A pending Activity target is superseded/replaced when production has been removed, rather than waiting indefinitely.

**Corrections verified:** `make check` passed (829 tests), as did [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39320777630), reconcile and platform verification; both checker jobs succeeded on the corrected image. The independent read-only review found its blockers addressed. Real Git history accepted the throwaway's exact staging Release and rejected variants with tag `999-abcdef0` or a changed uid.

**First promotion, PR [#86](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/pull/86), console job #30:** its six files were only `prod/release.yaml`, `prod/instance.yaml`, `prod/events.sops.yaml` and `prod/offers/`. It was unlabelled and said a person merges it. Release equalled staging's; production's NATS public key and egress port differed from staging's (compared, never printed). [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39321208421) passed. After review, `gh pr merge --merge --match-head-commit …` used GitHub's normal merge API (the same path as the button, not the replay gate), producing `12cd6e26` at 20:40:59 UTC. The deployed job page immediately showed `main has not rendered it yet`, not Live. Render's `4840dfef` followed at 20:41:20 (21 s), adding the production manifests, unit and registration. Validate on the records-only merge also [passed](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39321349516). Staging stayed byte-identical. Production became Healthy on build 1 and its worker logged `work done`; the deployed job page showed **Live in prod**, `swhurl-try-30/prod runs it.`

**Automatic later promotion, PR [#87](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/pull/87), console job #31:** app commit `dc545b3` added a `proof_revision: 2` startup-log field (local check/build and all 22 app tests passed). Its [build](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-try-30/actions/runs/39321419754) published `2-dc545b3` (`c19fd807…`); Deploy's `d9efc874` put it in staging, Healthy. The deployed review page returned 200 and showed a bounded rendered diff of one file and the optional Hold checkbox. PR #87 contained only `prod/release.yaml` and `prod/offers/source.yaml`, and was labelled `auto-merge`. [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39321725434) and the [gate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39321726198) passed. The gate waited 10 s for Validate and merged on the newest main as `74727b94` at 20:44:29, including the rendered HelmRelease in that one commit. Production's running digest matched build 2; Activity showed **Live in prod**. The console stayed within its 256Mi limit with zero restarts (observed at 110Mi during the first-promotion job, 42–44Mi between jobs; not a measured peak).

**Rollback held, PR [#88](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/pull/88), console job #32:** `b55d3329` reverted only staging's build-2 deployment (records, offers source and rendered HelmRelease) to build `1-3cbe7bb`; `app-sync` was a no-op, `make check` passed (829 tests), and staging became Healthy on build 1 while production stayed Healthy on build 2. The deployed review page identified a rollback, omitted the Hold checkbox and said it needs a manual merge. PR #88 contains only `prod/release.yaml` and `prod/offers/source.yaml` and opened unlabelled. [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39322342547) passed. Adding `auto-merge` deliberately exercised the gate: the [queued workflow run](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39322410956) failed as expected, removed the label and posted `Auto-merge refused: it promotes an older image than production runs (a rollback): a person reviews it`. The later label-triggered run succeeded with nothing to merge. At that checkpoint PR #88 was open and unlabelled, production remained on build 2, and the deployed job page said **Waiting for you**, naming the gate's refusal.

**Operator merge and completed rollback proof:** the operator merged PR #88 in GitHub at 21:01:06 UTC as `ade7392f`. [Render](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39324115607) committed `c38c6d4f` at 21:01:28 (22 s), changing only production's rendered HelmRelease. [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39324115800) passed on the records-only merge. `make flux-reconcile` and `make verify-platform` passed; `make app-status APP=swhurl-try-30 ENV=prod` showed Healthy at `c38c6d4f`, running build `1-3cbe7bb` with its expected digest (`b9dc9318…`). The live HelmRelease's image and render hash matched the committed rendered rollback. The deployed console's job #32 showed **Live in prod**, `swhurl-try-30/prod runs it.`, not Superseded. Its persisted first-seen time was 21:02:40 UTC, after Render's commit, not the healthy-old-image interval. B2's live exit is met. The throwaway is a private worker with no database or app Secret; it remains in staging and production. Deploy may move staging back to its newest image on a subsequent dispatch or its hourly run; the held PR's production Release stays the reviewed build 1 regardless. The real signed-in Google browser path, concurrent newer staging/tooling while a promotion is open, the first-promotion `app-secret` command and a lost-push retry have not been exercised live (offline coverage exists for the latter cases).

**Entry points:** a direct request to the deployed console through localhost port-forward without an identity returned 401; with a test identity header, the first-promotion review returned 200 and correctly offered a manual merge. This is not evidence of a browser's real Google sign-in. Public HTTP redirected to HTTPS (301); signed-out HTTPS redirected to Google (302).

**B2 size, including review corrections:** net diff `981e1050..47bc584e` (`git diff --numstat`, counting comments/blank lines): production tooling, templates and CSS +469/−389 (net +80), no workflow changes; tests +540/−268 (net +272); all code +1009/−657 (net +352). Documentation and generated/live manifests are excluded. The rendered-diff preview, merge-render retry path and Activity handling outweighed the removed promotion machinery; B2 is not a net line-count reduction. Removed `fingerprint`, `inputs_hash`, `metadata`, `body_metadata`, `control`, `review_metadata`, `replay_promotion`, `stages.control`, the promotion-control route, the `swhurl-promotion` marker, `--expect-config`, the `promotion-review-required` label, Hold/Request auto-merge buttons, and `Setting.later`. New-app replay and its comparison code remain until B3.

## Record-based app validation (19 October 2026)

`d28e8ce6` removes rendered `env-drift`, `per_environment.py` and its allowlist tests. Strict App/Release/Settings parsing and the existing records-to-manifests comparison own validation; `store.world` checks workload kind and start command agree across an app's Release records. Explicit `check-apps <path>` now checks selected manifests against their records too, loading dependency records without checking unrelated manifests or shared files. Production keeps independent Settings; no first/later promotion implementation changed. Making Settings travel with promotion and B3 were out of scope.

**Offline:** `make check` passed (826 tests, 18 policy instances including fixtures, repository/schema/documentation checks, collector checks and lint); `make check-templates` passed for all seven supported stack/type combinations and both invalid worker/database refusals. Regression tests cover sign-in removal, image replacement, an extra route, a service-account token and a handwritten replica count even with no production sibling; kind/command disagreement; explicit-path checks; a newer staging release and independent environment settings. The first full test run exposed copied live Reloader/registry lists in scratch fixtures; those setups now describe only their own apps. Independent read-only review found no blockers.

**Live:** [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39328501899) passed and pinned console image `src-3ebf2bae0e67bb0c` in `f7e8e0cd`. `make flux-reconcile` succeeded; `make verify-platform` passed, confirmed the current console tooling and recent successful dashboard/checker jobs, with one warning: `swhurl-try-30` staging still runs build 1 while its image policy advertises build 2, following the preceding B2 rollback proof. No app was redeployed to clear that unrelated warning. `make app-status APP=<app> ENV=<env>` for every live instance showed all 15 Healthy. The first verification script ended nonzero only because its final `make app-status` omitted the required arguments; the explicit per-instance rerun succeeded. No new browser/sign-in or live mutation exercise was performed for this validation-only change.

**Size:** `git diff c57ac1a9..d28e8ce6 --numstat -- tools` is production Python +34/−246 (net −212), counting comments and blank lines. Removed `flatten`, `exposure_of`, `source`, `drift` and the entire rendered-key/file exception table. Generated app manifests are unchanged.


## Records-only new-app PRs offline (19 October 2026)

[Plan section 24](plan.md#records-only-prs-decision-b-operator-19-october-2026-stages-started-19-october-2026), **Offline checkpoint; [live exit completed 20 October below](#records-only-new-app-prs-live-20-october-2026)**. This checkout implements records-only console creation: App, staging Release and Settings without an egress port, generated encrypted events key, and offers input documents (API, events and flags). Direct CLI creation still generates and writes its complete rendered tree. The gate shares promotion's merge/render/check/push-retry path, validates strict new-app records and key shape/recipients/public allocation, refuses existing apps, cross-app/generated paths, preallocated ports and automatic calls/subscriptions, and relies on policy for platform-owned images. It does not replay commands or fetch app declarations at merge time. Activity treats Render's system port allocation as completion of the same creation, including following the seed to its own build.

**Offline verification:** focused commands, all under `pi-durable-subagents hold machine --`: `PYTHONPATH=tools uv run --frozen python tests/run.py -k test_console_merge` (26 tests), the same with `-k test_console_changes` (77 tests), and `-k test_console_stages` (14 tests), all passed; `uvx ruff@0.16.9 check tools tests` passed. The new tests run actual app-new generation, records-only filtering, merge-tree simulation with FakeRunner and actual app-sync rendering: two PRs from one base both merge with unique ports and all unit registrations; a lost push race retries within the same run after a third creation takes a port; offers and the original key survive; malformed/missing records/keys, public key, allocated port, overwrite and cross-app path refusals; policy rejects a foreign image; failed merge/render/check commands or label removal never push. A real `Change.open` through the fake GitHub API asserts the exact committed paths and record/key/offer contents, while separately proving direct CLI output remains rendered and allocated. Complete-tree Activity tests cover a manual records-only merge, subsequent allocation/render, following an own build, and supersession by an operator settings change.

Initial `make check` runs passed all 828 tests, app policy for 18 instances, schema rendering, collector checks and lint, but failed the documentation link check while this evidence heading/link was being added (first absent, then a mismatched heading slug). The final `pi-durable-subagents hold machine -- make check` rerun passed: 828 tests, app policy for 18 instances, validation for 36 active render entrypoints, collector checks and lint. Existing collector deprecation and Starlette test-client warnings are unchanged. No templates, app types, workflows, shell scripts or layout changed. The initial verification omitted `make check-templates`; it is required because `app-new` changed and is included in the review-correction checks below.

**Not exercised here:** any live operation, deployed-console/browser entry point, real GitHub merge or push race, concurrent live creation, or cluster/key decryption. The parent owns independent review, deployment and the approved public `swhurl-try-31`/`swhurl-try-32` concurrency proof. No repository/package was created, no remote or cluster mutation was performed, and no commit or push was made by this implementation session. Earlier dated evidence is unchanged.

**B3 size, including review corrections:** implementation diff `9f65b6ae..32e03170` (`git diff --numstat -- tools tests`, counting comments and blank lines): production Python +126/−202 (net −76); tests +547/−286 (net +261); all code +673/−488 (net +185). Documentation is excluded; no generated manifests or workflows changed. Removed `replay_new_app`, `GATE_FLAGS`, `REQUEST`, `request`, `request_marker`, `scope`, `same_file`, the production `changed_files` comparison helper, the `swhurl-request` argv marker and the replay-only `app-new --events-key` option. Separate `merge_promotion`/`merge_new_app` paths are replaced by `merge_records`; the test-only tree-diff helper is not production comparison machinery.

**Independent review corrections:** reviewer `01M5B2VE3QH2J86AP308MTFB8K` blocked (P0) new-app records granting themselves policy exceptions and (P1) arbitrary job schedules bypassing CLI validation. The gate now holds any nonempty `settings.exceptions` for manual review before rendering. The regression uses `exposure: public`, the default shared-cookie-domain host and `exceptions: exposure=not actually reviewed`, and proves no push. The workload renderer now validates the effective job schedule using `contract.schedule_problem`, preserving the default; any explicit schedule on a non-job is refused. Regressions build arbitrary records, not CLI arguments: invalid ranges, zero steps and non-job schedules fail actual rendering and the gate's actual sync with no push; `app-sync` on manually merged records exits 2 without writing any files or registration. All four new test methods failed before the production fixes and pass afterwards. Focused reruns under the machine lease passed `test_console_merge` (28 tests), `test_app_model` (17 tests), and Ruff. The post-correction `pi-durable-subagents hold machine -- make check` passed (832 tests, 18 policy instances, 36 active render entrypoints, collector checks and lint). `pi-durable-subagents hold machine -- make check-templates` passed all seven supported stack/type combinations in staging and production and both invalid worker/database refusals.

**Source-audit corrections approved by the parent:** compared `new.validate` with strict records, rendering and policy. Team membership and alerts have policy checks, web port/health paths have strict records, and exposure/image pins have policy checks (policy exceptions are now held). The parent confirmed that the three further findings restore existing B3 restrictions: the gate now holds reserved app name `notify`, reusing `swhurl.apps.calling.NOTIFY` (the actual constant `new.py` uses); `model/secrets.apply` uses `contract.secret_key_problem` before annotating or referencing a Secret; `model/sqlite.apply` rejects worker/job databases before allocation. No CLI behavior was relaxed or new validation framework added. Tests use strictly parsed arbitrary records to exercise invalid, reserved and duplicate secret names, worker/job databases with and without allocations, actual sync refusal without file/registry writes, and gate no-push paths. Focused checks under the machine lease passed `test_console_merge` (30 tests), `test_app_model` (19 tests), and Ruff. The final restriction-restoration `pi-durable-subagents hold machine -- make check` passed (836 tests, policy for 18 instances, validation for 36 active render entrypoints, collector checks and lint). `pi-durable-subagents hold machine -- make check-templates` passed all seven supported stack/type combinations in both environments and both invalid worker/database refusals. Independent rereview found no remaining code blockers and independently passed 213 focused tests covering the gate, model, console changes, Activity and the existing app contracts, manifests, jobs and promotion. Deployment and the live proof subsequently passed, as recorded below.

## Grants recorded by the callee (20 October 2026)

[Plan section 24](plan.md#grants-recorded-by-the-callee-operator-20-october-2026), **B4a grant model**. A call connects only when the caller's `calls` names the callee and the callee's `allows` names the caller or its current team (`team:<team>`); a call without a grant, a grant without a call and a call to an absent app render nothing for the pair ([calling another app](apps.md#calling-another-app)). New `make app-allows`; team rename rewrites `team:<old>` grants in the same render as the relabel; team removal is refused while a grant names the team; first promotion copies staging's `allows`; `make check-apps` reports a `team:` grant naming an unregistered team.

**Deploy order:** the parser commit `10bb5962` (accepts `allows`, renders nothing from it) deployed first; Validate's bot pinned console `src-7230ac92a6c62fa1` (`aafb4fac`), and its pod ran digest `1d94f4b6…` Ready with zero restarts from 07:23:17 UTC, before any record held `allows`.

**Migration, no render change:** a one-off script wrote the grants today's calls imply: `team:demo` on `demo-orders` (staging, prod) and `demo-shop` (staging); `swhurl-try-11`, `swhurl-try-12` on `swhurl-try-10` (staging, prod; different team); `team:insights` on `swhurl-try-12` (staging, prod). `git diff` showed only those seven `instance.yaml` files; a second `app-sync` wrote nothing and `make check-apps` passed for 20 instances. The `team:insights` grant also names `swhurl-try-30` to `-32`, which call nothing, so it is unused for them.

**Checks:** `make check` passed (839 tests). One earlier full run failed `test_backup_job.SqliteJobTests.test_a_database_that_is_not_one_or_is_missing_publishes_nothing` (exit 0 for a non-database file under the parallel run); it passed three times alone and on the full rerun. It is the intermittent failure seen on the plan commit; not investigated further.

**Live at `69817ca2`:** [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions) and Render passed for `15911b05`; Validate's bot pinned console `src-4c33d03e03b133de` (`69817ca2`), whose pod runs digest `68f84ce9…`, Ready with zero restarts. `make flux-reconcile` and `make verify-platform` passed with no BAD or WARN lines; Flux applied `69817ca2`. The live `SWHURL_ALLOWED_CALLERS` are unchanged from before the migration: `demo-orders` staging and prod `demo-shop`, `demo-shop` staging `demo-shopper`, `swhurl-try-10` staging and prod `swhurl-try-11,swhurl-try-12`, `swhurl-try-12` staging and prod `swhurl-try-11`. `make app-status` reported all 17 instances Healthy. Not exercised live: `make app-allows` on a real instance, an ungranted call, a team rename or removal with grants, and a first promotion carrying `allows` (offline tests cover each).

The correction copies Render's pinned v0.9.0 install block, including its SHA-256 verification and path export, under Deploy's existing `deploy == 'true'` condition. The first run failed only the test's two old hint assertions; those were updated, and both subsequent full runs passed. Existing collector component-name and Starlette test-client deprecation warnings remain. The exact new Deploy make command also passed locally. Both workflow YAML files parsed; the install `run` blocks compare exactly. Executing the copied install block in a temporary directory verified the download checksum, extraction, `GITHUB_PATH` export and executable version `0.9.0`. No shell scripts, Makefile, layout, app types or templates changed; the additional bash syntax loop and local template checks were not required.

No app image, grant, credential, repository, package or retained data was deliberately changed for this proof. No signed-in browser check was performed. `todo.txt` and `docs/platform-capability-catalogue.md` were left untouched. M1's proposed refusal to remove an app while any `calls` names it, even if waiting for a grant, and any live throwaway removal still need the operator's confirmation.

## Model review follow-ups M2 (20 October 2026)

[Plan section 29](plan.md#29-model-review-follow-ups--started-20-october-2026), **M2 done**. `4f6a6fe7` makes one model sync plan own complete shared-file updates (unit registrations, Reloader namespaces, NATS, egress and notification routes) and makes `Create`, `Update` and `Remove` explicit operations through `prepare`; `apply` only executes prepared writes and deletions. This finishes B4b (sync deletes the managed output, unit file and registry line of an instance whose records are both absent) and B4c (team PRs carry `apps/teams.yaml`, relabelled `app.yaml` files and renamed grants; Render writes the routes; channel PRs stay held).

**CI regression and fix:** [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39500185965) failed on `4f6a6fe7` with 11 test failures: release updates allocated a NATS key whenever NATS exists, which needs `sops`, absent on the runner. [Render](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39500185904) passed with nothing to render, console deployment was skipped and cluster manifests did not change. `205d01aa` restores the pre-M2 rule (the condition is identical to `release.py` at `24c3458a`): ordinary updates allocate a key only for publications or subscriptions; first production and `app-sync`'s key bootstrap ask for one explicitly. A regression test fails before the fix and passes after it. Locally, `make check` with `sops` hidden from `PATH` (as on the runner) passed **885 tests** with exit 0; `git diff --check` passed. The implementation's independent rereview cleared all its findings; the queued review of `205d01aa` itself did not run (its provider's usage window was exhausted).

**CI and live:** [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39508059002) and [Render](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39508058761) passed for `205d01aa`. The parent also ran real SOPS bootstrap and rotation in a temporary checkout with a new throwaway age key, proving exact destination-rule/recipient selection, seed-to-public-key agreement after decryption and zero preparation writes; no platform credentials were used or printed.

**Offline limits:** this checkpoint precedes the [verified deployment](#model-review-follow-ups-m5-live-21-october-2026). M7a is **+46**, M7b **−20** production lines. Across M0–M7 against original review `fb553653`, `tools/swhurl` is **20,144 → 19,971, net −173** (all `tools/` Python **20,186 → 20,017, net −169**). No production code moved outside tools; the narrowed M7 does not claim the original deletion estimate.

**Limits:** backend identity-header checks are not real Google-signed-in browser interaction. No valid app change, pending relation, promotion, removal, deliberate webhook drift/create/update, notification delivery to a subscriber, credential rotation, repository/package creation, data deletion, backup-destination change, template/app-type revision or flux-system configuration change was exercised live. ## Model review follow-ups M8 live (21 October 2026)

[Approved scope](plan.md#m8-scope-and-acceptance-approved-21-october-2026), **M8 done**. Implementation `95b40a59` gives Render, Deploy and the console's merge gate one way to push to `main`: `bots.push_main` (a fresh worktree of the newest `main`, the change, `make check-repo check-apps`, one commit, a non-forced push; a lost push starts again on the newer `main`; a refusal or failed check pushes nothing). Internal commands `bot-render` and `bot-deploy` replace the two bash retry loops; `merge_records` passes its merge as the change. `platform/backup/mongodb.yaml`'s `spec.suspend` is the one backup switch: `store.platform` and the console's `render_targets` read it through `backup.suspended`, `backup.SUSPENDED` is deleted, and a present `platform/backup/` with a missing or malformed file is an error.

**Offline:** `make check` passed **967 tests**, lint and repository checks; host/fixture `bash -n` and `git diff --check` passed. No template or app-type change, so `check-templates` was not run. New tests: 8 for `bots.py` (lost push then retry, every push lost, nothing to change, failed check showing its output, refusal from `change` or `ready`, Deploy's partial failure, Render's message, and one real-Git race against a local bare repository that lands on top of the other writer), 4 for the switch (repository value, scratch root, malformed/missing values, rendered jobs follow it, `check-apps` disagreement) and 1 that the console's targets read the same switch. All 43 existing merge-gate tests pass unchanged except the patched name of the check function.

**CI and rollout:** [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39679828655) passed every job and pinned console/notify image `src-c9ce7970da5ad2fa` in `84a19727`. [Render](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39679828799) passed but ended at its pre-check (`main` already rendered), so `bot-render` did not run. `make flux-reconcile` applied `84a19727` to all **37 Flux units**; `make verify-platform` passed with **101 OK**, no BAD or WARN; `make app-status` reported all **17 instances** Healthy. Console and notify deployments run the new image; scheduled jobs `console-dashboards-29876977` and `console-notifications-29876977` succeeded on it. All 7 backup CronJobs (MongoDB and six SQLite) remain unsuspended. Signed-out HTTP returned **301** and HTTPS **302** for `/`, `/activity` and `/new`. A manual [Deploy run](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39680349558) ran `bot-deploy` live and ended with `Nothing changed.`, pushing nothing.

**Size:** against `1d8dcbeb`, production Python in `tools/swhurl` is **+226/−73, net +153** (**19,971 → 20,124**; B1 +33, A1 +120); workflow YAML **+7/−55, net −48**; tests **+230/−3, net +227**. This exceeds the scope's "about zero" estimate; the operator accepted it before commit. Across M0–M8 against original review `fb553653`, `tools/swhurl` is **20,144 → 20,124, net −20**.

**Limits:** the push paths of `bot-render`, `bot-deploy` and the merge gate have not run live: they need a merged held PR, a records-only push or a new staging image. Their retry and refusal behaviour is proved offline only. Suspending backups through the one switch was not exercised live. Activity latency, real Google-signed-in interaction and the other limits recorded for M7 are unchanged. `todo.txt` and `docs/platform-capability-catalogue.md` are untouched.

## Console Git read performance (21 October 2026)

**Approved first step, delivered:** `a23b5300` adds one request-owned platform-file reader to app details: one GitHub client through template rendering, one resolved `main` commit, one answer per path (including absence and failures), at most four file reads in flight, and concurrent independent related-app reads. Existing record parsing, relation evaluation and offer-document caches remain. PR/promotion checks retain their own readers. Diagnostic logs time page files, render-target cache hits/misses, archive download/extraction/model-render and the complete Activity handler. [Canonical behaviour and timing definitions](console.md#read-performance). Activity loading and progress rules are unchanged; there is no persistent cache or cross-request in-flight sharing.

**Offline:** final `make check` passed **980 tests**, lint, policy and repository checks; `git diff --check` passed. Eleven new reader tests cover concurrent result/absence/failure sharing, pinned commits and special-character paths, failed head lookup without moving-main fallback, per-request retry and isolation, nested-batch concurrency, injected readers, invalid records, client closure and template-time reads. Two render-target tests cover safe timings for success and each failed phase, including temporary-tree cleanup. Independent read-only review and rereview found no remaining blockers. No template, app-type, script, Makefile or layout changes; `check-templates` and the host/fixture syntax loop were not required.

**CI and rollout:** [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39684079637) and [Render](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39684079781) passed. Validate pinned console/notify image `src-32ef77a2a765c05d` in `1bca6339` (digest `ac9afe1f1cc32c623a981de0325ad0f4703300fd13603f9aa50a92a24f2e64a6`), pulled before verification. `make flux-reconcile` applied it to all **37 Flux units**, all Ready. `make verify-platform` passed with **101 OK**, no BAD or WARN; `make app-status` reported all **17 instances** Healthy, with desired/applied `1bca6339` and running images matching desired. Console was Ready with zero restarts; dashboard and notification jobs `29877012` succeeded on the new image. Console memory was 66Mi at one later observation, not a measured peak. No ERROR/CRITICAL records appeared in the observed rollout/application log window.

**Backend timing sample:** five sequential rounds before deployment, then the same five rounds after deployment through temporary localhost Service port-forwards, using a hand-set test identity header. Order each round: health, Activity, Deployments, demo-shop staging details, Catalogue, Overview. All returned 200. These are time-to-first-byte seconds; response-body download generally added little. The first read is reported separately from the median of the following four, not as a percentile or a controlled cold-start benchmark. The old process's prior cache state was not established; the new process's first Activity read is confirmed below to have render-cache misses.

| Page | First before | First after | Median rounds 2–5 before | Median rounds 2–5 after |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Health | 0.007 | 0.005 | 0.009 | 0.006 |
| Activity | 58.834 | 60.402 | 1.302 | 1.346 |
| Deployments | 3.431 | 3.304 | 0.178 | 0.147 |
| demo-shop/staging | 11.379 | 7.368 | 2.796 | 1.690 |
| Catalogue | 1.525 | 0.140 | 0.074 | 0.066 |
| Overview | 0.501 | 0.466 | 0.425 | 0.492 |

App details' four later samples were **2.793, 2.799, 3.196, 2.492 s** before and **2.004, 1.513, 1.748, 1.632 s** after: about **40% lower median** in this small sample, not a general performance guarantee. Its new reader logged **31 unique paths, 2 shared hits, 0 failures** on the first read, and **7 unique paths, 2 hits, 0 failures** on each later read, plus one commit resolution per page. The first read includes uncached offer documents; warm documents remain cached. Independent offline assertions prove each requested path is fetched once at the same commit and the file-reading client closes after rendering.

**Activity diagnosis, not a fix:** the first new-process request logged **28 concurrent archive reads** (27 remembered merge commits plus current `main`), totalling **31.86 MB**. Individual download phases took **32.19–57.75 s** (median **48.49 s**), extraction **0.15–0.67 s**, and model/render/cleanup **0.003–0.250 s**. All ended `phase=done`; **12 trees had zero record-based instances**. The complete handler took 60.396 s. The following four measured requests hit the immutable render cache and answered in **1.21–1.62 s**. This run localises the long first load to archive acquisition rather than rendering, but does not establish why GitHub/codeload was slow. Activity is not claimed faster. Deferring historical evidence and sharing in-flight immutable reads are next candidates requiring their own scope; no persistent storage is justified by this measurement alone.

**Entry points and limits:** external HTTP returned **301** and signed-out HTTPS **302** on `/`, `/activity`, `/apps/demo-shop/staging` and `/new`. Through the port-forward, `/`, `/activity`, `/apps`, `/platform`, `/teams`, `/new`, `/catalogue`, `/egress`, both demo-shop environments and staging promotion review returned **401** without identity and **200** with the test header. A wrong-Origin scale POST returned **403** before starting a job; no valid mutation was submitted. Port-forwards stopped and their port was no longer listening. These checks are not a real Google-signed-in browser session. No app change, repository/package creation or deletion, data deletion, credential rotation, backup-destination change or flux-system configuration edit was performed. Existing deprecation warnings remain outside scope; `todo.txt` and `docs/platform-capability-catalogue.md` are untouched.

## Model review follow-ups M9 live (23 October 2026)

[Approved scope](plan.md#m9-to-m14-scope-and-acceptance-approved-23-october-2026), **M9 done**. Implementation `5fa1e51f`:

- **One meaning of "applied" (C1).** `InstanceStatus.applied` (revision equality) is now `at_newest`; `AppRow.applied` is now `applied_revision`. `Progress.applied` is the one field named so: the intended render reached the cluster. `Track`, `Progress` and `MERGED` say in their docstrings what each is.
- **One file-equals-render test (C2).** `generated.same(path, text)` compares bytes. The writer (`write.plan`), the instance and NATS users checks, the egress proxy (both checks) and the notification routes check use it; `write._same` and `generated.normal` are deleted, and `generated.file_problems` names the differing document paths, or reports "formatted differently" when only the bytes differ. A scratch `app-sync` with byte comparison rewrote nothing: all **133** instance files and the 3 shared files of this checkout, and the **19** files of `tests/fixtures/apps`, equal their render byte for byte. The frozen sample `tests/fixtures/instance` had two `helmrelease.yaml` files equal as documents but not as bytes (key order); they were rewritten to their rendered bytes.
- **Records check their own grammar (C3).** `write.prepare` re-parses the App, Release and Settings of every created or updated instance before anything is written. `transitions.changed_list` refuses a name given twice (to the list, `--add` or `--remove`), and `app-calls` and `app-allows` now use it. `set_events`' subscription grammar check and `set_allows`' allow grammar check are deleted.
- **F1 leftovers.** `policy.evaluate` reads `published` from `Release.parse(release.yaml)`; the unused `instance.instances()` is deleted.

**Deliberate behaviour changes:** `check-apps` and the commands' guard fail on a file equal to its render as data but not as bytes (`make app-sync` rewrites it). `app-calls`/`app-allows` refusals read `already there:` / `not there to remove:` like `app-egress` and `app-events`; `app-allows --add x --add x` is refused instead of deduplicated. Grammar errors come from the record parser (`calls: not allowed: Bad`).

**Offline:** `make check` passed **983 tests** (one fewer: the deleted function's test); `make check-templates` passed all **7 app types** in staging and production and both refusals; `git diff --check` passed. Independent read-only review found no blocker; its two should-fixes (a stale `InstanceStatus.applied` in AGENTS.md and `set_allows`' repeated grammar check) were applied before commit.

**CI and rollout:** [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39976872249) and [Render](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/39976872435) passed. Validate pinned console/notify image `src-abdaa0fa9fc64322` in `1a836c90`. `make flux-reconcile` applied it to all **37 Flux units**; `make app-status` reported all **17 instances** Healthy. The console runs the new image with zero restarts; `console-dashboards-29879480` and `console-notifications-29879480` succeeded on it. `make verify-platform`: **100 OK, 1 BAD**, the same ClickHouse merge memory failures recorded for [M9.0](#model-review-follow-ups-m90-live-23-october-2026) (33 in the last hour).

**Size:** against `24f182c4`, production Python in `tools/swhurl` is **+70/−86, net −16**, short of the −20 to −40 budget; tests **+24/−34, net −10**.

**Remaining beside the new paths:** the console's form checks (`console/merge.py`) still apply `NAME_RE`, `SUBSCRIPTION_RE` and `ALLOW_RE` to form input before building a command; `teams.py` and `release.py` compare their own files by bytes without `same` (neither is a render of the records). A Release with an empty image repository parses but does not round-trip, so a command would refuse that instance; rule `platform-image` already fails such a record.

## Model review follow-ups M10 live (23 October 2026)

[Approved scope](plan.md#m9-to-m14-scope-and-acceptance-approved-23-october-2026), **M10 done**. Implementation `0b82990c`:

- **Alerts as one value (C4).** `set_alerts(root, app, choice)` takes `Thresholds(changes) | NoAlerts() | DefaultAlerts()`; `make app-alerts` keeps `--none` and `--default` in an argparse mutually exclusive group and refuses thresholds mixed with either, or nothing (exit 2). The `sum(...) != 1` check is deleted.
- **Rotation is an operation.** `write.Rotate()` is an `Update` that clears the instance's NATS identity; normal allocation makes the new pair. Preconditions: the instance exists, has a complete identity, still needs one after the change, and (deliberate change) its files are what its records render (`guard`). `rotate=` is deleted from `prepare`, `allocate` and `write.sync`; `app-sync --rotate-nkey` passes `{target: Rotate()}`.
- **One NATS key rule.** The first step found why `app-sync` gave every instance a key: once NATS exists every instance needs one (its NATS user carries its feature-flag permissions, and `check-apps` fails an instance without one). The operator chose that as the rule for every operation; the `bootstrap=` mode is deleted. This reverses the M5 contract that an ordinary update leaves an unrelated missing identity absent: in a tree with such an instance any command now gives it its key in the same change (and needs `sops`). A healthy tree has none: a full `write.prepare` of this checkout with a FakeRunner (any external call fails) planned **0 writes, 0 deletions and 0 external calls** for **17 instances**, all keyed, so console PRs, the merge gate, Render, Deploy and Validate need no `sops`.
- **`app-new --no-register` is deleted.** Its two callers (`tests/fixtures/apps.sh`, `test_app_manifest.py`) run in roots without a registry, which `plan` already leaves alone; nothing changes.

**Offline:** validated in a clean worktree holding only the M10 files on `1dce9dbb` (another session's uncommitted v2 work excluded): `make check` passed **983 tests**; shell syntax (the fixture script changed) and `git diff --check` passed. The tests that asserted the old split now assert the one rule; tests of first-key creation start from a tree without keys (`with_units(keyed=False)`); a new test shows a rotation also gives another instance its missing key. Independent read-only review (retried after the provider's session limit stopped the first attempt) found no blocker; its cuts (`Rotate` as an `Update`, docstrings, help text without "bootstrap", exit-code assertions) were applied.

**CI and rollout:** [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/40025736905) and [Render](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/40025736682) passed. Validate pinned console/notify image `src-f70848644950ebb3` in `c3622c2c`. `make flux-reconcile` applied it to all **37 Flux units**; `make app-status` reported all **17 instances** Healthy. The console runs the new image with zero restarts; `console-dashboards-29879841` and `console-notifications-29879841` succeeded on it. `make verify-platform`: **100 OK, 1 BAD**, the ClickHouse merge memory failures recorded since [M9.0](#model-review-follow-ups-m90-live-23-october-2026) (19 in the last hour). No identity was rotated live.

**Size:** against `1dce9dbb`, production Python in `tools/swhurl` is **+78/−48, net +30**, against a budget of −15 to −40; the operator accepted the overrun before commit. The plan's own shape costs it: three alert choice classes, the `Rotate` operation and the identity precondition, while the deleted modes were a few short lines. Tests **+88/−56, net +32**.

## Model review follow-ups M12 live (23 October 2026)

[Approved scope](plan.md#m9-to-m14-scope-and-acceptance-approved-23-october-2026), **M12 done**. Implementation `5d1f1c1a`:

- **Gate rules as a table (C7).** `merge.STAGING_REVIEW` gives each Grants and Choices field a reason and a review class (`changed`, `added` or `free`). `settings_held` loops over the table in its own order, not `dataclasses.fields` as the scope said, so that when several fields change the first reason shown is the one it was before (exceptions, then who can reach it, then added callers, then added hosts); allocations are still compared whole first. A test fails when a Grants or Choices field has no row, checks every row's verdict for a change from the defaults, that taking access away merges itself, that production is always held and which reason wins when several fields change.
- **Promotion.** `first_production` keeps its keep-list. A test runs it on a staging instance with every field set and requires each Settings field to be either copied (exposure, calls, allows, exceptions, issuer, replicas, capacity, schedule) or production's own (host, subscriptions, egress and every allocation). Note: it copies Choices whole, so a new Choices field would be promoted by default; the test fails until the field is classified.
- **Change identity.** `changes.creation_inputs(settings)` is the one hash input of `render_targets`; a test shows every Settings field changes it except the allocated egress port.

**Offline:** validated in a clean worktree holding only the M12 files: `make check` passed **986 tests** (3 new); `git diff --check` passed. Independent read-only review found no blocker and confirmed `settings_held` gives the same result as before for every input; its doc fixes and test guards (unique field names across the parts, every `added` field a tuple, every example value away from its default) were applied.

**CI and rollout:** [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/40030529501) passed; its deploy job (24 s) pinned console/notify image `src-096de94fd798b1df` in `62e7fcc2` through `bots.push_main`. The console runs the new image with zero restarts; `console-dashboards-29879882` and `console-notifications-29879882` succeeded on it. `make verify-platform`: **100 OK, 1 BAD**, the ClickHouse merge memory failures recorded since [M9.0](#model-review-follow-ups-m90-live-23-october-2026) (20 in the last hour).

## Model review follow-ups M13 live (23 October 2026)

[Approved scope](plan.md#m9-to-m14-scope-and-acceptance-approved-23-october-2026), **M13 deployed; signed-in browser acceptance pending**. Implementation `ca5ac377`:

- **GitHub and proposals.** `console/changes.py` is deleted, with no compatibility module: `github.py` owns the client and GitHub readers; `proposal.py` prepares and publishes changes; `stages.py` owns `render_targets` and `creation_inputs` beside `Target`. All importers and test patch targets use the new modules. The proposal-to-promotion import stays lazy to avoid a cycle.
- **Route groups.** `server.create_app` constructs a frozen, 23-field `Context` and gathers routes from `routes/pages.py`, `instance.py`, `teams.py`, `activity.py` and `new_app.py`. Shared caches remain per app; request-scoped file readers still span rendering and reset in `finally`. There is no second handler path beside the moved handlers.
- **Route ordering adjustment.** Grouping changes the route order, not the multiset of 26 path/method/name entries. The overlapping promotion GET stays before the generic instance-change POST; a new test pins that order. The static mount moves last without overlapping another current route.

**Offline and review:** in an isolated worktree excluding unrelated v2 work, the parent ran `make check`: **987 tests passed**, including lint and repository checks; shell syntax and `git diff --check` passed. All seven split modules import independently in fresh processes. Independent read-only review found no issues: all 22 original `changes.py` definitions and 66 original `server.py` definitions are AST-equivalent after normalizing moved module references; it checked closure bindings, cache/request lifetimes, route entries, imports and response/job/log handling. The first review provider hit its quota; the replacement completed. The committed patch exactly matched the validated tree, and unrelated unstaged edits and untracked files were preserved.

**CI and rollout:** [Validate](https://github.com/samclement/swhurl-platform/actions/runs/40034509862) passed every job, including templates; its deploy job (21 s) pinned console/notify image `src-1919480abf769c08` in `13a21ea8`. A local watcher incorrectly reported failure because its GitHub lookup used an abbreviated commit SHA and found no run; CI itself succeeded. `make flux-reconcile` reconciled all **37 Flux units**; `make app-status` reported all **17 instances Healthy**. The console runs the new image with **zero restarts**; `console-dashboards-29879920` and `console-notifications-29879920` succeeded on it, followed by successful `29879921` jobs. Signed-out `/`, the `demo-orders/staging` page and its promotion review return **301 over HTTP / 302 over HTTPS**; HTTPS POST promotion also redirects to sign-in (**302**). `make verify-platform`: **100 OK, 1 BAD**, the pre-existing ClickHouse merge memory failures (19 in the preceding hour), not changed here.

**Size:** against `fa3e437f`, production Python is **20,309 → 20,500, net +191** (**+1,415/−1,224**), exceeding the original 0 to +50 estimate. The added imports, module headers, route factories and explicit shared context account for the overrun. It was disclosed before the operator instructed review and commit if ready; the grouped layout and verbatim handler bodies were retained rather than compressing them to meet the estimate.

**Not exercised:** real Google-signed-in pages and action forms, live mutations, new-repository creation and job resumption. Browser acceptance must check Overview, Deployments (including map view), Catalogue, Teams, Platform, Egress, a unit page, staging and production app pages, promotion review, Activity, a job/log page and New app; inspect the app's access/caller/egress/connect/flag/schedule/scale/removal forms, Teams/channel forms, catalogue Use, unit controls and any applicable Finish creating form. Opening forms does not verify their live submission. New app creates a real public repository; do not submit it or destructive controls merely to inspect the split.

## ClickHouse metric_log daily partitions (24 October 2026)

Symptom: `make verify-platform` reported 19 to 57 failed merges an hour on the `otel_*` tables since 23 October (code 241, server limit 3.60 GiB), and ClickHouse used about 0.9 cores. Those merges peaked at 64 to 185 MiB; they failed because one other merge took the server to the limit. `system.text_log` showed it: the vertical merge of all of October in `system.metric_log` (11 Wide parts, 2.12M rows, 1,350 columns, about 580 MiB) failed 2,400 to 4,200 times an hour since 03:00 on 23 October, each attempt raising merge memory to 2.2 to 3 GiB for a second. `system.part_log` recorded none of those failures, so the check counted only the other tables. The 7-day TTL had not applied either: deleting rows rewrites the whole part and failed the same way every 4 hours (`merge_with_ttl_timeout`), so the table still held 29 September.

Applied at `2703fca6`: `metric_log` gets `partition_by: toYYYYMMDD(event_date)` and `ttl_only_drop_parts = 1` (accepted offline by `clickhouse-server:25.7.8.71-alpine` first). The operator restarted ClickHouse at 09:44:07 UTC; the new table carries both. The renamed old table `metric_log_1` kept retrying its October merge (124 failures in 2 minutes) even with `ttl_only_drop_parts`, so with the operator's approval it and `metric_log_0` (51k rows, from 29 September) were dropped at 09:47 UTC: ClickHouse's own metrics before 09:44 are gone, and HyperDX's ClickHouse page starts there.

Ten minutes later: no merge error in `system.text_log` or `system.part_log`, 195 merges succeeded, tracked memory peaked at 804 MiB (merges 354 MiB), ClickHouse 277m CPU. `make verify-platform`: **100 OK, 1 BAD**, the 37 failures before 09:46:19 still in its one-hour window. `make app-status`: all **17 instances** Healthy. Not yet seen: a day dropped whole by its TTL (the first on 31 October) and the merge check reporting OK after the window passes. `verify-platform` still reads only `system.part_log`.

## v2 and code graph push (24 October 2026)

Pushed `8bad66d1..be041cf4` (8 commits): code graph steps 0 to 3 and its `view`, the `AGENTS.md` refactoring steps, the v2 phase-1 kernel, and v2's [guardrails](iteration-2-plan.md#guardrails) with `make check-v2` in CI. None of it reaches a Flux path; the `check-v2` entry in `tools/swhurl/commands.py` changed the console image inputs. Validate run `40137705613` passed every job, including `make check-v2` on Go 1.26.0, and pinned console image `src-5ec440c4f6f75fcd` (`0fa00601`). `make flux-reconcile` applied it; `make verify-platform`: **101 OK**, no warnings (the console runs the new image); `make app-status`: all **17 instances** Healthy. Locally one `make check` run in about seven failed in `test_backup_job` (`test_a_database_that_is_not_one_or_is_missing_publishes_nothing`: exit 0 instead of 1) and passed alone and on rerun; the pushed commits do not touch that test or `backup_job.py`. Cause, found 25 October: the test's `setUp` left its WAL connection to the garbage collector, and closing it checkpoints the WAL into the file, so a collection after the test had overwritten the file restored a valid database (7 of 300 runs failed, none with collection disabled or forced first). The writer connection is now kept and closed explicitly, before the corruption in that test: 0 of 900 runs failed, and the copy test reliably reads rows from the WAL.

On 25 October, pushed `d84e269f` (the v2 change report and the record of the phase-1 review): Validate run `40169361728` passed every job and pinned console image `src-bb67b3ea5e9ba65f` (`989f1bf6`); `make flux-reconcile` applied it; `make verify-platform`: **101 OK**, no warnings; `make app-status`: all **17 instances** Healthy.

On 25 October, pushed `ca028e21` (the phase-1 review fixes and that record): Validate run `40171140752` passed every job, including `make check-v2`; the console image was unchanged, so no deploy commit followed; `make flux-reconcile` applied it and `make verify-platform` reported **101 OK**, no warnings.

On 25 October, pushed `2eaeaea4`, then `1262960f` and `ad334338` (the backup test fix; v2's phase-2 compiler and its review fixes): Validate runs `40173423834` and `40176357867` passed every job; the second fetched the chart v2's profile pins and ran `make check-v2` with `REQUIRE_HELM=1`, so the compiler's manifest check ran. Neither changed the console image. `make flux-reconcile` applied each; `make verify-platform` reported **101 OK**, no warnings, after each.

## First v2 instance live (25 October 2026)

`swhurl-try-v2-1` in staging is owned only by v2: its six files are `swhurl-v2 compile --out` output for `rel-sha256:c0f1fdc2f20cf221cb9b163bafe844ec7eed3ed3c1de37e329e499a7a2058c6b` (source `samclement/swhurl-try-11` at `d771828acf7bc301ed79df502f75c5866c65b774`, digest `sha256:63a4e954464150c9574981bd54900b46872f9e98ba0fb0a535702662462ccc5d`), and no v1 command, record or unit generated any of them. Pushed `58331a98` then `7ef3d79e`; `make flux-reconcile` applied both.

**Three findings, in the order they appeared:**

1. **The first image was wrong for the model, not for the compiler.** The instance was first pinned to `demo-orders`, which keeps a SQLite database; v2 generates no storage (a deferred row), so the pod crash-looped on `unable to open database file` and the Helm install timed out. `swhurl-try-11` (web, no database, no publishes) runs: `1/1 Running` on the pinned digest, `ResourceQuota pods 1/14`.
2. **A timed-out install leaves a release Flux cannot remediate.** The retry reported `Stalled=True … MissingRollbackTarget: cannot remediate failed release`, because a failed *install* has no revision to roll back to; the unit then failed its health check even though the replacement pod was healthy. `flux reconcile helmrelease swhurl-try-v2-1 -n swhurl-try-v2-1-staging --force` reset the counters and the release became `Ready=True Released=True`.
3. **NetworkPolicy egress is enforced, but not from the instant a pod exists.** From inside the app pod (`kubectl debug`, sharing its netns) `https://1.1.1.1`, `example.com` and even the cluster DNS service were unreachable — deny-all works. But a *new* pod wearing the same selector reached `https://example.com` (`HTTP 200`) in its first seconds, and the same pod was refused after 75 s. So the policy takes some seconds to program for a new pod; a v2 instance can egress until then.

**Verified live:** the running pod's image is the compiled digest; the unit and HelmRelease are Ready; a sibling pod in the same namespace cannot reach the service (`HTTP 000`, connect failed); a pod in `default` cannot reach it across namespaces; every one of the 37 other units stayed `True`; `make verify-platform` passed (one WARN: the console image was older than the checkout, because Validate had failed twice — see below); all v1 instances unchanged.

**Removed the same way, at the operator's confirmation (25 October 2026):** deleting the five instance files, the unit file and its line in `clusters/home/kustomization.yaml`, then pushing, was the whole removal — no command, no `kubectl`, no v1 record. `make flux-reconcile` completed its wait for the first time in this exercise, with every unit Ready. Flux pruned the namespace `swhurl-try-v2-1-staging`, the unit, the HelmRelease (`True: Helm upgrade succeeded`, three Helm release Secrets), the ResourceQuota and the NetworkPolicy; no object anywhere carries the name, managed namespaces went from 29 to 28, the units from 39 to 37 (`check-repo` entrypoints 39 to 38), and all 37 remaining units are Ready. `make verify-platform` passed. `demo-orders/staging` stayed `Healthy` on its own newer digest (`sha256:e2a5b5cf…`), so no v1 instance moved.

**CI caught what local checks could not:** both pushes failed Validate, so the console was not deployed. `test_docs.test_this_repository_passes` failed on the runner because the status page cited `354aae1e`, a hash my rebase had rewritten: `git cat-file` says it exists locally (the reflog keeps it) but no ref reaches it. The check now requires the cited commit to be reachable from `git rev-list --all`, which is what a reader of the repository has, and the page cites the current hash. That fix then failed for the opposite reason: the tooling job checked out with a depth of 1, so no citation could resolve; it now fetches full history and a shallow clone warns instead of failing. The next run (`795c1176`) passed every job and deployed console image `src-597131bf01433b14` as `6483d6f8`; `make verify-platform` then passed with no WARN.
